### MARKET BOARD

_731 markets listed · 646 executable books rendered · 85 no-real-market placeholders suppressed · 0 silently omitted._

### CORRELATION GROUPS

- `2026_04_MIA_MIN::SCORING_UP` (same, strong (qualitative)): game total over, either team total over, QB passing yards over, receiver yards/receptions over, anytime TD yes, total TDs over — These rise and fall together with game scoring and pace.
- `2026_04_MIA_MIN::MIN_SIDE` (same, strong (qualitative)): MIN moneyline, MIN spread, MIN team total over, MIN player props over — Team-side markets share the same game-script risk.
- `2026_04_MIA_MIN::GAME_SCRIPT_OPPOSED` (opposed, moderate (qualitative)): favourite rushing volume over, favourite passing volume over — A team that leads runs more and throws less; volume props on the same team can conflict.

2. De'Von Achane (RB, MIA) is ruled out. Is the market's price on his replacement's usage already reflecting the redistributed role, or still anchored to a committee?
